# Ablation matrix

> **Eval render.** Every row is scored on the current render of eval_r2 (`data/eval_r2_relabel`, re-made from the crest-audit relabelled manifests), the render every score in [`r6/`](r6/) and [`r7/`](r7/) uses. Checkpoints: the ablations were re-scored on a laptop ([`r6_local/`](r6_local/), `scripts/rescore_local.ps1`); `vaani_full_r4`, `vaani_full_r4_ctl`, `vaani_full_r3_e32`, `gtcrn_finetuned` and the tier46 cascade on the training box ([`r6/`](r6/)). `tier46_anchor` and `tier46_anchor_v2` are frozen copies of `vaani_full_r3_e32` and `vaani_full_r4_ctl` and score identically to them.

> Not in this file: the external baselines (raw, NLMS, RNNoise, DeepFilterNet3, GTCRN pretrained, H-GTCRN) and WER, which were only measured on the earlier render; see [`matrix_prerelabel.md`](matrix_prerelabel.md). The two renders differ at 606 of the 617 nominal items and every checkpoint scores 0.29-0.48 dB lower SNR_out here, so compare rows within one file, not across them.

PESQ: wideband P.862.2 @16 kHz (`pesq` package). P.862 is withdrawn by ITU in favour of P.863; reported because the brief requests it.
SNR_out = 10log10(||s||^2/||s_hat-s||^2) vs clean primary (distortion counts as error). SI-SDR reported separately.
Targets (problem statement): SNR_out>15 dB, STOI>0.85, PESQ>2.5 - marked per bucket row and per overall nominal row.
Legend: ✓ = lower 95% bootstrap bound exceeds target; ~ = mean exceeds target but lower bound does not; ✗ = mean does not exceed target. Intervals resample evaluation items for one checkpoint; they do not measure training-seed variability or give a joint three-target guarantee.
